## Surveys

| Date | Resource | Environments | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 2026‑05‑08 | [Securing Computer-Use Agents: A Unified Architecture-Lifecycle Framework for Deployment-Grounded Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07110) | web, desktop, cross-platform | Survey of computer-use agent reliability and security across browsers, desktops, and mobile apps, using architecture and lifecycle views to map failures and controls. |
| 2024‑11‑17 | [Generalist Virtual Agents: A Survey on Autonomous Agents Across Digital Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.10943) | web, desktop, android, windows | Surveys virtual agents for browsers, applications and operating systems, organizing prior work by task type, observation and action spaces, model strategies and evaluation methods. It examines screen-based interaction and keyboard, mouse and touch control, alongside transferability and long-sequence decision-making challenges. |

## Papers

| Date | Resource | Environments | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 2026‑11‑04 | [MKD2World: A Typed Spatial MarkDown World Model For Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06544) | web, desktop | Introduces MKD2World, a desktop web world model that represents interface elements in Typed Spatial Markdown and predicts post-action states, supported by AtomBlock-WebUI annotations. |
| 2026‑10‑30 | [Hybrid Computer Use: A Unified Programmatic, Semantic, and Visual Interface for General Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41432) | desktop | Introduces Hybrid Computer Use (HCU), a desktop agent interface combining code, accessibility data, and GUI actions, with AX diffs reporting action effects. |
| 2026‑10‑29 | [Mage-VL-Cowork: A Full Training Recipe to Learn Hybrid Computer Use From Evolved Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40747) | desktop, web, cross-platform | Introduces Mage-VL-Cowork, a computer-use agent trained for GUI, code, and terminal actions, with Mage-CUA-Forge generating desktop and web tasks for training. |
| 2026‑10‑29 | [VeriCUA: Verification as Agentic Exploration for Computer-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40578) | desktop, web, cross-platform | Introduces VeriCUA, a cross-platform task verifier that gathers evidence through GUI or code checks to assess computer-use agent completion. |
| 2026‑10‑25 | [HobbyLM: A Sparse Mixture-of-Experts Model Family on a Hobby Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34582) | desktop, macos | Describes HobbyLM, a small model family whose post-trained variants include a computer-use agent and an on-device macOS computer-use adapter. |
| 2026‑10‑20 | [CUA-Recovery: Benchmarking Error Recovery in Long-Horizon Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27645) | desktop | CUA-Recovery benchmarks error detection and recovery across desktop applications, using agent failures to create test states and training trajectories. |
| 2026‑10‑19 | [Agent World Model in the Wild: Computer-Use Agent Environment Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26828) | web, desktop, linux | WildAWM synthesizes resettable GUI/MCP/CLI environments and cross-interface tasks, using execution-based checks of solvability, difficulty and verifier consistency before supervised and reinforcement learning. |
| 2026‑10‑19 | [Comparing Computer Use Behavior Across Multiple Levels of Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26703) | desktop, web | MAP annotates computer-use traces at linked operation, function/consequence, method and goal levels, then compares human and agent workflows through sequence, pattern and effort metrics. |
| 2026‑10‑19 | [CUA-Lite: An Open Platform for Computer Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26071) | desktop, linux, windows, web, android | CUA-Lite unifies environment interfaces, supervised data formats and per-model harnesses for evaluation, reinforcement learning and supervised fine-tuning across desktop, browser and mobile tasks. |
| 2026‑10‑19 | [VeriCUA: Learning Proactive Verification for Reliable GUI Agent Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26469) | desktop, linux, web, android | VeriCUA trains a Judge to select verification checks and stopping decisions while a frozen Probe inspects the post-task GUI. Single-attempt reinforcement learning balances verdict correctness and Probe use. |
| 2026‑10‑16 | [Adaptation of Computer Use Agents to New Software with User Manuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23012) | desktop | MAGE retrieves software-manual sections into fixed execution plans and generates setup/main task-plan pairs whose GUI rollouts support application-specific policy adaptation with round-based GRPO. |
| 2026‑10‑15 | [LEASH: Uncertainty-Aware Weak-to-Strong Escalation and Oversight for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22280) | desktop | Uses weak-agent uncertainty to decide when to escalate to a stronger model and monitor its subsequent task execution. |
| 2026‑10‑15 | [OpenGCUA: Toward a Fully Open Stack for Generalist Computer Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22200) | desktop | Introduces an open generalist computer-use stack combining visual and programmatic interaction, with model-controlled observation and task-state decisions. |

| 2025‑05‑22 | [ARPO:End-to-End Policy Optimization for GUI Agents with Experience Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16282) | desktop | ARPO extends group-relative policy optimization with a replay buffer that reuses successful GUI trajectories across training iterations. | 2025‑04‑07 | [On the Robustness of GUI Grounding Models Against Image Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04716) | web, desktop, cross-platform | Evaluates GUI grounding models under natural image noise, untargeted adversarial perturbations, and targeted image attacks.
